Full log-gradient local search algorithm:
```
def log_gradient_update(v, tau, num_trials):
    a=exp(clip(v,-8,8)); S=sum(a); c=conv(a,a); r=c/S^2; p=softmax(tau,r); A=fft_cross(a,p); D=dot(p,c); h=4*n*a*(A/S**2 - D/S**3);
    h = h - h.mean()
    norm = sqrt(mean(h^2))
    if norm small: return v,False
    direction = -h/norm
    for alpha in [0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01]:
        v2 = v + alpha*direction
        v2 = v2 - v2.mean()
        # maybe clip v2 to [-8,8]
        score2 = objective_full(v2, smooth=False) # exact F maybe 0 if invalid
        if score2 < score - tol: accept
    if best not found, maybe annealing.
```

Function `score_and_grad_v(v, tau)`: 
```
v_c = np.clip(v, -12,12)
a = np.exp(v_c)
S = a.sum()
if S<=0: return inf, None
fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)[:2n-1]
# clip tiny negative
max_c = c.max()
score = 2*n*max_c/(S*S)
r = c/(S*S)
m = r.max()
if tau >0:
   z = r - m
   # scale to avoid underflow; since z<=0
   expv = np.exp(tau*z)
   sumexp = expv.sum()
   p = expv / sumexp
else:
   p = np.zeros(2n-1); p[max_indices]=1/k
# cross A:
fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
A = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fp), N)[:n]
# Due numerical, A small neg? a nonnegative p nonneg -> A should nonneg, but set max(0)
A = np.maximum(A,0)
D = np.dot(p,c)
h = 4.0*n*a*(A/(S*S) - D/(S*S*S))
h = h - h.mean()
return score,h
```

def local_search(v0, initial_score, deadline, label):
    v = v0 - v0.mean()
    score = initial_score
    best_v = v.copy(); best_score=score
    step_sizes = [1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05,0.02]
    tau = 5000
    no_improve=0
    while time<deadline-time_slice:
       score,h=score_grad(v,tau)
       if not finite: v=best_v; score=best_score
       h_norm = np.sqrt(np.mean(h*h))
       if h_norm < 1e-12: break
       direction = -h/h_norm
       # maybe add small random if stuck
       improved=False
       for alpha in step_sizes:
          v_cand = v + alpha*direction
          v_cand = v_cand - v_cand.mean()
          v_cand = np.clip(v_cand, -8, 8)
          sc = exact_score(v_cand)
          if sc < score - 1e-12:
             v=v_cand; score=sc; improved=True; break
       if improved:
          if score < best_score: best_v=v.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
          tau = min(max(tau*1.1? maybe), 50000) # focus
       else:
          no_improve +=1
          if no_improve == 50:
             # random perturbation or try different tau
             tau = ...
             v = best_v + rng.normal(0,0.05)
             score=best_score
             no_improve=0
          if no_improve > 500: break
```

Line search details:
```
def local_search(v, score, deadline):
    v = v - v.mean(); v = rescale_range(v, 8)
    best_v=v.copy(); best_score=score
    tau = 20000
    base_step = 0.05
    no_improve=0
    while time<deadline:
        cur_score = score
        cur_score, h = score_grad(v,tau)
        if cur_score < best_score: best...
        if h is None: break
        hn = rms(h)
        if hn < 1e-10: break
        dirx = -h/hn
        # maybe use a minimum step lower
        tested=False
        for step in (base_step, 0.5*base_step, 0.25*base_step):
            v2 = v + step*dirx
            v2 = v2 - v2.mean()
            v2 = rescale_range(v2,8)
            s2 = score_v(v2) # exact max only
            if s2 < cur_score - 1e-12:
                v=v2; score=s2; tested=True
                base_step = min(base_step*1.3, 0.5)
                break
        if not tested:
            # try increasing if base small? maybe random
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve % 50 == 0:
               # try perturbations from best
               v = best_v + rng.normal(0, 0.1, n).cumsum? no, small frequency noise
               score=best_score
               base_step=0.05
            if no_improve > 300: break
            base_step = max(base_step*0.7, 0.005)
        # adjust tau
        if tested: tau = min(tau*1.2, 100000)
        else: tau = max(tau*0.8, 2000)
    return best_v,best_score
```

Functions:
- `next_power_of_two(x): return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length()`
- `make_fft_size(n): return 1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()`
- `fft_convolution(a, N):` uses rfft.
  ```
  N = max(N, 1)
  fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
  c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
  c = c[:2*len(a)-1]
  np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
  return c
  ```
- `exact_score_from_a(a):`
  ```
  n=len(a); S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12 return inf
  N=fft_size maybe global? To avoid computing each call, pass N.
  c=fft_convolution(a,N)

But we need N in functions. Could use global `GL_N` set in propose. Helper top-level can read global. Is that okay? Yes. But if multiple calls, okay. Alternatively pass N argument. Many calls, passing N easy. Define `def score_from_a(a, N):`.

- `score_and_direction(v, K, N):`
Input v length n. It should internally center/clip v, compute a, S, c, r, p topK, A, h, score. Return score,h, maybe a,S,c? To avoid recompute for candidate line search, we only need score candidate and current h. Candidate score maybe recompute inside. Okay.
  ```
  v = np.asarray(v); v = v - np.mean(v); v = np.clip(v, -8,8)
  a=np.exp(v)
  S=a.sum()
  if not finite: return inf,None
  fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
  c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
  S2=S*S
  r=c/S2
  max_r=r.max(); score=2*n*max_r
  K=min(K, 2n-1)
  idx=np.argpartition(r, -K)[-K:]
  p=np.zeros(2n-1); p[idx]=1.0/K
  fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
  A=np.fft.irfft(fa*np.conj(fp),N).real[:n]
  A=np.maximum(A,0)
  D=float(np.dot(p,c))
  term = A/S2 - D/(S2*S)
  h = 4.0*n*a*term
  h[h<...]=...
  h -= h.mean()
  # maybe clip
  h = np.clip(h, -1e6,1e6)
  return score,h
  ```
`score` here exact using max_r; can also use `2*n*max(c)/S2`.

def score_from_v(v,N):
    v = np.clip(v - v.mean(), -8,8)

    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)[:2*n-1]

```

LP function:
```
def lp_update(f, M=None):
    n=len(f); S=f.sum(); f=f/S
    if M is None:
       c=conv(f); M=c.max()
    # build A_ub: 2n-1 x n. A_ub[k,i]=f[k-i] if k-i in [0,n-1] else0
    # Use scipy.sparse: for each i, f_i contributes to rows k=i..i+n-1 value f[i]. Could use lil?
    rows=[]; cols=[]; vals=[]
    for i in range(n):
       fi=f[i]
       if fi==0: continue
       rows.extend(range(i, i+n)); cols.extend([i]*n); vals.extend([fi]*n)
    A = csr_matrix((vals,(rows,cols)), shape=(2*n-1,n))
    res=linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=M, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')
    b=res.x
    # rescale sum to S=1? Since f sum1, if sum b>0
```

Parametric a shape definitions top-level:
```
def add_shape(cands, a, n):
   if a is None: return
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.shape!=(n,): return
   if not finite or any a<0: clip
   amin=a.min()
   if amin<=1e-12: a=np.maximum(a,1e-12)
   # normalize not needed for v
   v=np.log(a)
   v=v-v.mean()
   v=np.clip(v,-8,8)
   cands.append(v)
```
Shapes:
- ones.
- linear: 1+c*(x-0.5), c [0.5,1,2,4,-2,-4]. Ensure positive via clip.
- exp: np.exp(c*(x-0.5)), c [-2,-1,-0.5,0.5,1,2].
- cos: 1+c*np.cos(np.pi*x), c [0.3,0.6,0.9,1.2] (clip small).
- cos2: 1+c1 cosπx + c2 cos2πx for grid; also sin: +c3 sinπx.
- cos half: np.cos(np.pi*x/2)**p +eps, p [1,2,3,4,0.5?].
- (1-x)^p + eps, p [0.5,1,2,3,4].
- x^p + eps.
- U: 1 + c*(x-0.5)^2.
- Sin ramp: 1+c*np.sin(np.pi*x) (center high? symmetric, maybe bad).
- Double hump asymmetric: 1 + c*np.exp(-((x-d)/w)^2) with d near0.2,0.5,0.8, w 0.1,0.2,0.4. Positives. Ensure nonneg.

Pseudo:
```
def local_search(v, score, N, n, K, deadline, rng, max_iter=100000):
    v = np.array(v, float).copy(); v = v - v.mean(); v = np.clip(v,-8,8)
    best_v=v.copy(); best_score=score
    base_step=0.05
    tau? no topK
    k_current=K
    no=0
    iter_count=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline and iter_count<max_iter:
       sc, h = score_and_direction(v, k_current, N, n)
       if not finite: v=v.copy(); sc=best_score; ...
       if sc < best_score: best...
       # if exact score vs current slight difference? okay
       hn = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(h*h)))
       if hn <= 1e-10:
          # stuck; random perturb
          v = best_v + rng.normal(0,0.05,n)
          v = v - v.mean(); v = np.clip(v,-8,8)
          score = best_score
          continue
       direction = -h/hn
       # try steps: base, base/2, base/4, 2*base
       tried=[]
       for step in (base_step, 0.5*base_step, 0.25*base_step, 2*base_step):
           v2 = v + step*direction
           v2 = v2 - v2.mean(); v2 = np.clip(v2,-8,8)
           s2 = score_from_v(v2, N, n)
           if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < sc - 1e-12:
               v=v2; score=s2
               best update
               no=0
               base_step = min(0.2, base_step*1.25)
               break
       else:
           no+=1
           base_step = max(1e-4, base_step*0.7)
           if no>200:
              # random smooth perturbation from best
              v=best_v + make_random_perturbation(n, rng, amp=0.2)
              score=best_score; no=0; base_step=0.05
           if no>2000: break
       # adapt K
       if no==0:
           k_current = min(64, k_current*2)
       else:
           k_current = max(4, k_current//2)
       iter_count+=1
    return best_v,best_score
```
But adjusting base_step after no improvements maybe; when found, increase. If base grows too large, line search tries 2*; okay. v range clipped.

Random smooth perturbation helper: generate v noise via low-freq random Fourier:
```
def random_smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp):
    m=8
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    out=np.zeros(n)
    for j in range(1,m+1):
       a=rng.normal(0,amp/j); b=rng.normal(0,amp/j)
       out += a*np.cos(np.pi*j*x)+b*np.sin(np.pi*j*x)
    return out
```

Let's sketch code:
```
import numpy as np
import time

DOCSTRING...

def _next_pow2(x):
   x = int(max(1,x))
   return 1 << (x-1).bit_length()

def _fft_setup(n):
   N = _next_pow2(3*n - 2)
   return N

def _score_a(a, N, n):
   S = float(np.sum(a))
   if S <= 1e-12 or not np.isfinite(S): return np.inf
   fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
   c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
   c = c[:2*n-1]
   np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
   sc = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))/(S*S)
   if not np.isfinite(sc): return np.inf
   return sc

def _score_v(v, N, n):
   v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
   if v.shape != (n,): return np.inf
   v = v - float(np.mean(v))
   v = np.clip(v, -8.0, 8.0)
   a = np.exp(v)
   if not np.isfinite(a).all(): return np.inf
   return _score_a(a, N, n)

def _direction_v(v, K, N, n):
   ... return score,h
```

Upsampling candidate:
```
def _upsample_v(v_small, n_small, n_full):
    if len(v_small)==n_full: v=v_small.copy()
    else:
       xs=np.linspace(0,1,n_small); xf=np.linspace(0,1,n_full)
       v=np.interp(xf,xs,v_small)
    v=v-v.mean(); return np.clip(v,-8,8)
```

Top-level helper for random perturb: 
```
def _random_smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp, max_modes=10):
   out = np.zeros(n)
   if n <=1: return out
   x = np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   for m in range(1, max_modes+1):
       ca = rng.normal(0.0, amp / float(m))
       sa = rng.normal(0.0, amp / float(m))
       out += ca * np.cos(np.pi * m * x) + sa * np.sin(np.pi * m * x)
   return out
```
This allocates x each call; okay. Could precompute? Not.

Block perturbation:
```
def _random_block_perturb(v, rng, amp):
   n=len(v); width=int(rng.integers(max(2,max(2,int(n//50))), max(3,int(n//8)))); start=int(rng.integers(0,n-width+1)); v=v.copy(); v[start:start+width]+=rng.normal(0,amp,width); return v
```
No lambda.

In local_search reset, try a list of perturb amplitudes and types.

If local min for max, any active peak subset nonnegative weights sum yields gradient maybe not zero unless p in dual cone. Our topK equal weights may not be in that cone, so a single Newton step could improve? If local min, any descent direction should exist? For nonconvex local min, no descent first-order within feasible. So gradient likely no exact improvement. To find global better, need barrier annealing/random restarts. We can do many random smooth perturbations from best and local search; 2400s maybe enough. But if local landscape has deep global at 1.4, random restarts may find.

Increase random starts: after full local on top candidates, if not beating target, run many small-scale random smooth perturbations of best accepted by local? Current local already resets randomly. Time left can continue best with more perturbations.

Maybe use simulated annealing: temperature allow uphill moves for e.g. 0.01, using p topK. Could escape. We can modify line search to accept if s2 < sc + temp when no_improve large. Temperature decays. Let's implement an annealing flag:
- `anneal_tol = 0.0` initially; when no_improve > 50, set `anneal_tol = max(0.01, 0.05*best_score)`.
- For candidates, if min_score < sc - eps accept; else if min_score < sc + anneal_tol accept (uphill). Mark uphill. If accepted uphill, count; if more than 50 consecutive uphill and no best improvement, reset to best.
This may cross barriers. We need carefully not blow time. Implement maybe.

```
def _local_search(v, score, n, N, K_start, deadline, rng, print_every=1000):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    v = v - float(np.mean(v)); v = np.clip(v,-8,8)
    best_v = v.copy(); best_score = float(score)
    score = float(score)
    K = int(K_start)
    base = 0.05
    no_improve=0
    stuck=0
    it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < 100000:
        sc, h = _direction_v(v, K, N, n)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            v = best_v.copy(); sc = best_score
            if np.isfinite(sc): ... else break
        if sc < best_score:
            best_v = v.copy(); best_score=sc; no_improve=0
        hn = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(h)))) if h is not None else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(hn) or hn < 1e-12:
            # move by random perturb from best
            vq = best_v + _random_smooth_perturb(n, rng, 0.2)
            vq = vq - vq.mean(); vq=np.clip(vq,-8,8)
            s_q = _score_v(vq,N,n)
            if np.isfinite(s_q) and s_q < best_score + 0.05:
                v=vq; score=s_q
            else:
                break
            it+=1; continue
        direction = -h/hn
        # try step sizes
        steps = (base, 0.5*base, 0.25*base, 1.5*base)
        improved=False
        # if stuck, allow moderate uphill from local? maybe no
        for st in steps:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
            v2 = v + st*direction
            v2 = v2 - float(np.mean(v2)); v2 = np.clip(v2,-8,8)
            s2 = _score_v(v2,N,n)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < sc - 1e-12:
                v=v2; score=s2; improved=True; no_improve=0
                best update? Already if sc? update below
                break
        if improved:
            base = min(0.2, base*1.25)
            K = min(64, K + max(1,K//4))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            base = max(1e-4, base*0.75)
            K = max(4, K - max(1,K//8))
            if no_improve > 60:
                # random reset from best
                accepted=False
                for amp in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8):
                    vq = best_v + _random_smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp)
                    # optional block
                    vq[block...] maybe
                    vq = vq-vq.mean(); vq=clip
                    s_q = _score_v(vq,N,n)
                    if np.isfinite(s_q) and s_q < best_score + 0.03:
                        v=vq; score=s_q; no_improve=0; base=0.05; accepted=True; break
                if not accepted:
                    v=best_v; score=best_score; no_improve=0; base=0.05
                    if no_improve_total? break? We'll use a persistent `restart_count`
                    restart_count +=1
                    if restart_count > 20: break
        if it % print_every == 0 and it>0:
           print(f"local it={it} best={best_score:.6f} cur={score:.6f} K={K}")
        it +=1
    # at end if current better, best already
    return best_v, best_score
```

Detailed helper implementations:

```
def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()
```

`_candidate_from_a`:
```
def _candidate_from_a(a, n):
    try:
       a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size != n:
       return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-10)
    # if all zero? set ones
    if not np.any(a > 0): return np.zeros(n)
    v = np.log(a)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    if not np.isfinite(v).all(): return None
    v = np.clip(v, -8.0, 8.0)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    return v
```
If a has zeros, log -> -inf? We clip to 1e-10 before log, fine.

`_score_a`: Use `a = np.asarray(a,float); if shape`. It doesn't center; v function does. Could use direct for exact final? internal.
```
def _score_a(a,N,n):
   a = np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.shape[0]!=n: return inf
   if not finite all: return inf
   a = np.maximum(a,0.0)
   S = float(np.sum(a))
   if S <=0: return inf
   fa = np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
   c = c[:2*n-1]
   np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
   sc = 2.0*n*float(np.max(c))/(S*S)
   return sc if np.isfinite(sc) else np.inf
```

`_score_v`:
```
def _score_v(v,N,n):
   v=np.asarray(v,float)
   if v.size != n: return inf
   v = v - mean; v=clip(-8,8)
   if not finite: inf
   a=np.exp(v)
   return _score_a(a,N,n)
```

`_direction_v`:
```
def _direction_v(v,K,N,n):
   v=... center clip
   a=np.exp(v)
   S=sum; if bad: inf,None
   fa = rfft(a,N)
   c = irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
   max(c,0)
   S2=S*S; S3=S2*S
   r = c/S2
   m = r.max(); score=2*n*m
   if not finite: inf,None
   L=2*n-1
   if K >= L: p=np.full(L,1.0/L)
   elif K <=1: p=zeros; p[argmax(r)]=1
   else: idx=argpartition(r, L-K)[-K:]; p=zeros; p[idx]=1.0/K
   fp=rfft(p,N)
   A=irfft(fa*conj(fp),N).real[:n]
   A=np.maximum(A,0.0)
   D=float(np.dot(p,c))
   term=A/S2 - D/S3
   h=4.0*n*a*term
   h=np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
   h = h - float(np.mean(h))
   rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(h))))
   if rms ==0 or not finite: ...
   limit = max(1e6, 100.0*rms)
   h = np.clip(h, -limit, limit)
   return float(score), h
```
If K=0, set1.

`_random_block_perturb`:
```
def _random_block_perturb(v, rng, amp):
    v = np.asarray(v,float).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n <4: return v
    lo = max(2, int(n // 200))
    hi = max(lo+1, min(n, int(n // 10)))
    width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi+1))
    width = min(width, n)
    start = int(rng.integers(0, n-width+1))
    if amp>0:
       v[start:start+width] += rng.normal(0.0, amp, size=width)
    return v
```
If hi maybe n, okay.

`_safe_mean_center_clip`:
```
def _center_clip(v, bound=8.0):
   v = np.asarray(v,float)
   if v.size ==0: return v
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)): v=np.nan_to_num(v)
   v = v - float(np.mean(v))
   v = np.clip(v, -bound, bound)
   v = v - float(np.mean(v))
   return v
```
Use everywhere.

`_upsample_v`:
```
def _upsample_v(v_small, n_small, n_full):
   v = np.asarray(v_small,float)
   if v.size == n_full: out=v.copy()
   else:
       xs=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,v.size)
       xf=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_full)
       out=np.interp(xf, xs, v)
   return _center_clip(out)
```

       a = 1.0 + c1*np.cos(np.pi*x) + c2*np.sin(np.pi*x)

   return cands

def _scale_sequence(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return [1.0]
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0); a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   m=float(np.max(a))
   if not finite or m<=0: return [1.0]*a.size
   a = a / m
   return [float(x) for x in a]
```

Pseudo code robust:
```
def _local_search(...):
    v = _center_clip(v.copy())
    best_v = v.copy(); best_score = float(score)
    score = best_score
    K = ...; Kmax=...
    base=0.05; no_improve=0; restarts=0; it=0
    while time... and it<...:
        sc,h = _direction_v(...)
        if not finite(sc) or h is None:
            v=best_v.copy(); sc=best_score
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score=sc; best_v=v.copy(); no_improve=0
        if h is None or not finite mean:
            # random restart
            accepted=False
            for amp in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4):
                vq=best_v + _random_smooth_perturb(n,rng,amp)
                vq=_center_clip(vq)
                s_q=_score_v(vq,N,n)
                if finite and s_q < best_score + 0.03:
                    v=vq; score=s_q; accepted=True; break
            if not accepted:
                v=best_v; score=best_score
            no_improve=0
            if not accepted: restarts += 1
            if restarts > 15: break
            it+=1; continue
        hn = ...
        if hn < 1e-12: similar restart
        direction = -h/hn
        improved=False
        for st in (base, base/2, base/4, base*1.5):
            v2 = v + st*direction; v2=_center_clip(v2)
            s2=_score_v(v2,N,n)
            if finite and s2 < sc - 1e-12:
                v=v2; score=s2; improved=True; break
        if improved:
            if score < best_score: best...
            no_improve=0
            base = min(0.25, base*1.25)
            K = min(K_max, K + max(1, K//4))
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            base = max(1e-4, base*0.75)
            K = max(4, K - max(1,K//8))
            if no_improve > 80:
                accepted=False
                for amp in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8):
                    vq=best_v + _random_smooth_perturb(n,rng,amp)
                    # every other block
                    if amp > 0.2:
                       vq = _random_block_perturb(vq, rng, amp*0.25)
                       vq = _center_clip(vq)
                    else:
                       vq = _center_clip(vq)
                    s_q = _score_v(vq,N,n)
                    if finite and s_q < best_score + 0.05:
                       v=vq; score=s_q; accepted=True; break
                no_improve=0
                base=0.05
                if not accepted:
                    v=best_v; score=best_score; restarts+=1
                    if restarts > 20: break
        if it % print_every == 0: print
        it+=1
    return best_v,best_score
```
This should work.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import operator

"""
Algorithm summary:
- Work in log-amplitude variables v, so a = exp(v) is always non-negative.
- The objective is scale invariant; v is periodically centered.
- Full-log variables are optimized with a projected gradient direction built
  from the top-K largest convolution coefficients (a smoothed/active-set
  surrogate for the exact maximum).
- Many smooth random/parametric initializations are generated at a reduced
  length, locally optimized, then upsampled to the target length.  If the
  provided height_sequence_1 exists, it is used as a strong full-length
  candidate and as a source for downsampled initializations.
- A final exact direct-convolution score (matching the evaluator) is used to
  pick the best valid sequence.
"""


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _center_clip(v, bound=8.0):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        return v
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    v = np.clip(v, -bound, bound)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    return v


def _candidate_from_a(a, n):
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size != n:
        return None
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-10)
    if not np.any(a > 0.0):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.log(a)
    v = _center_clip(v)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return None
    return v


def _score_a(a, N, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size != n:
        return np.inf
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    sc = 2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (S * S)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return np.inf
    return float(sc)


def _score_v(v, N, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size != n:
        return np.inf
    v = _center_clip(v)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.inf
    a = np.exp(v)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf
    return _score_a(a, N, n)


def _direction_v(v, K, N, n):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size != n:
        return np.inf, None
    v = _center_clip(v)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(v)):
        return np.inf, None

    a = np.exp(v)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf, None

    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf, None

    L = 2 * n - 1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:L]
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)

    S2 = S * S
    S3 = S2 * S
    r = c / S2
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(r)):
        return np.inf, None

    max_r = float(np.max(r))
    score = 2.0 * n * max_r
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return np.inf, None

    K = int(max(1, min(K, L)))
    if K >= L:
        p = np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=np.float64)
    elif K == 1:
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(r))] = 1.0
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(r, L - K)[-K:]
        p = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)

    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    A = np.fft.irfft(fa * np.conj(fp), N).real[:n]
    np.maximum(A, 0.0, out=A)

    D = float(np.dot(p, c))
    if not np.isfinite(D):
        D = 0.0

    term = A / S2 - D / S3
    h = 4.0 * n * a * term
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    h = h - float(np.mean(h))

    rms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(h))))
    if not np.isfinite(rms) or rms <= 1e-14:
        return score, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    limit = max(1e6, 100.0 * rms)
    h = np.clip(h, -limit, limit)
    return score, h


def _random_smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp, max_modes=10):
    out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 1 or amp <= 0.0:
        return out
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, int(max_modes) + 1):
        ac = float(rng.normal(0.0, amp / float(m)))
        sa = float(rng.normal(0.0, amp / float(m)))
        out += ac * np.cos(np.pi * m * x)
        out += sa * np.sin(np.pi * m * x)
    return out


def _random_block_perturb(v, rng, amp):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    n = v.size
    if n < 4 or amp <= 0.0:
        return v
    lo = max(2, min(n, int(n // 200)))
    hi = max(lo + 1, min(n, int(n // 10)))
    width = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
    width = int(max(2, min(width, n)))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
    v[start:start + width] += rng.normal(0.0, amp, size=width)
    return _center_clip(v)


def _upsample_v(v_small, n_small, n_full):
    v = np.asarray(v_small, dtype=np.float64)
    if n_full <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if v.size == 0:
        out = np.zeros(n_full, dtype=np.float64)
    elif v.size == 1:
        out = np.full(n_full, float(v[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    elif v.size == n_full:
        out = v.copy()
    else:
        xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(v.size), dtype=np.float64)
        xf = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n_full), dtype=np.float64)
        out = np.interp(xf, xs, v)
    return _center_clip(out)


def _append_from_a(cands, a, n, N):
    v = _candidate_from_a(a, n)
    if v is None:
        return
    sc = _score_v(v, N, n)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        cands.append((float(sc), v))


def _generate_candidates(n, N, rng, v_source=None, deadline=None):
    cands = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    _append_from_a(cands, np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), n, N)

    linear_coeffs = (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, -0.5, -1.0, -2.0)
    for c in linear_coeffs:
        _append_from_a(cands, 1.0 + c * (x - 0.5), n, N)
        _append_from_a(cands, np.exp(c * (x - 0.5)), n, N)
        _append_from_a(cands, 1.0 + c * np.cos(np.pi * x), n, N)
        _append_from_a(cands, 1.0 + c * np.sin(np.pi * x), n, N)

    for c1 in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9, 1.1):
        for c2 in (-0.5, -0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 0.5):
            _append_from_a(
                cands,
                1.0 + c1 * np.cos(np.pi * x) + c2 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x),
                n,
                N,
            )
            _append_from_a(
                cands,
                1.0 + c1 * np.cos(np.pi * x) + c2 * np.sin(np.pi * x),
                n,
                N,
            )

    powers = (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0)
    for p in powers:
        _append_from_a(cands, np.power(1.0 - x, p) + 1e-6, n, N)
        _append_from_a(cands, np.power(x, p) + 1e-6, n, N)
        _append_from_a(cands, np.power(np.cos(np.pi * x / 2.0), p) + 1e-6, n, N)
        _append_from_a(cands, 1.0 + float(p) * np.power(x - 0.5, 2), n, N)

    gaussian_centers = (0.05, 0.15, 0.3, 0.5, 0.7, 0.85)
    gaussian_widths = (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35)
    for cc in gaussian_centers:
        for ww in gaussian_widths:
            _append_from_a(
                cands,
                0.2 + np.exp(-np.power((x - cc) / ww, 2)),
                n,
                N,
            )

    random_limit = 300
    if deadline is not None:
        random_limit = max(50, random_limit)
    for _ in range(random_limit):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 9):
            amp = 0.7 / float(m)
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            a += float(rng.normal(0.0, amp)) * np.cos(np.pi * m * x + phase)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-4)
        _append_from_a(cands, a, n, N)

    random_v_limit = 200
    if deadline is not None:
        random_v_limit = max(40, random_v_limit)
    for _ in range(random_v_limit):
        if deadline is not None and time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        vv = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 9):
            amp = 0.9 / float(m)
            vv += float(rng.normal(0.0, amp)) * np.cos(np.pi * m * x)
            vv += float(rng.normal(0.0, amp)) * np.sin(np.pi * m * x)
        a = np.exp(np.clip(vv, -6.0, 6.0))
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
        _append_from_a(cands, a, n, N)

    if v_source is not None and np.asarray(v_source).size >= 2:
        vs = np.asarray(v_source, dtype=np.float64)
        if vs.size == n:
            vs2 = vs
        else:
            xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, vs.size, dtype=np.float64)
            vs2 = np.interp(x, xs, vs)
        vs2 = _center_clip(vs2)
        sc = _score_v(vs2, N, n)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            cands.append((float(sc), vs2))

    if not cands:
        cands.append((2.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)))
    return cands


def _local_search(v, score, n, N, K_start, deadline, rng, K_max=None, print_every=1000):
    v = _center_clip(np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).copy())
    best_v = v.copy()
    try:
        best_score = float(score)
    except Exception:
        best_score = np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 2.0
    score = best_score

    if K_max is None:
        K_max = min(128, max(16, n // 64))
    K = int(min(max(1, K_start), K_max))

    base_step = 0.05
    no_improve = 0
    restarts = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < 200000:
        sc, h = _direction_v(v, K, N, n)
        if not np.isfinite(sc) or h is None:
            v = best_v.copy()
            sc = best_score

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_v = v.copy()
            no_improve = 0

        if h is None:
            accepted = False
            for amp in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4):
                vq = best_v + _random_smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp)
                vq = _center_clip(vq)
                s_q = _score_v(vq, N, n)
                if np.isfinite(s_q) and s_q < best_score + 0.05:
                    v = vq
                    score = s_q
                    accepted = True
                    break
            no_improve = 0
            if not accepted:
                v = best_v
                score = best_score
                restarts += 1
                if restarts > 25:
                    break
            it += 1
            continue

        hn = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(np.square(h))))
        if not np.isfinite(hn) or hn < 1e-12:
            accepted = False
            for amp in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8):
                vq = best_v + _random_smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp)
                vq = _center_clip(vq)
                s_q = _score_v(vq, N, n)
                if np.isfinite(s_q) and s_q < best_score + 0.05:
                    v = vq
                    score = s_q
                    accepted = True
                    break
            no_improve = 0
            if not accepted:
                v = best_v
                score = best_score
                restarts += 1
                if restarts > 25:
                    break
            it += 1
            continue

        direction = -h / hn
        improved = False
        for st in (base_step, 0.5 * base_step, 0.25 * base_step, 1.5 * base_step):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline:
                break
            v2 = v + st * direction
            v2 = _center_clip(v2)
            s2 = _score_v(v2, N, n)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < sc - 1e-12:
                v = v2
                score = float(s2)
                improved = True
                break

        if improved:
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_v = v.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            base_step = min(0.25, base_step * 1.25)
            K = min(K_max, K + max(1, K // 4))
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            base_step = max(1e-4, base_step * 0.75)
            K = max(4, K - max(1, K // 8))

            if no_improve > 80:
                accepted = False
                for amp in (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.4, 0.8):
                    vq = best_v + _random_smooth_perturb(n, rng, amp)
                    if amp > 0.2:
                        vq = _random_block_perturb(vq, rng, 0.3 * amp)
                    else:
                        vq = _center_clip(vq)
                    s_q = _score_v(vq, N, n)
                    if np.isfinite(s_q) and s_q < best_score + 0.05:
                        v = vq
                        score = s_q
                        accepted = True
                        break

                no_improve = 0
                base_step = 0.05
                if not accepted:
                    v = best_v
                    score = best_score
                    restarts += 1
                    if restarts > 30:
                        break

        if it > 0 and it % int(max(1, print_every)) == 0:
            print(f"local_search it={it} n={n} best={best_score:.7f} cur={score:.7f} K={K}")
        it += 1

    return best_v, float(best_score)


def _v_to_sequence(v):
    v = _center_clip(np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64))
    a = np.exp(v)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return [1.0] * max(1, int(a.size))
    a = a / mx
    return [float(x) for x in a]


def _scale_sequence_array(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    mx = float(np.max(a))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / mx
    return a


def _score_sequence_direct(seq):
    a = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    n = int(a.size)
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if S <= 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    sc = 2.0 * n * mx / (S * S)
    return float(sc) if np.isfinite(sc) else np.inf


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """Search for a low-peak non-negative convolution sequence."""
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, budget - 12.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    n_full = 6962
    v_height = None
    height_seq = None

    h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if h is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        except Exception:
            arr = np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
        if arr.size >= 2:
            arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            vs = _center_clip(np.log(np.maximum(arr, 1e-12)))
            if 512 <= arr.size <= 20000:
                n_full = int(arr.size)
                v_height = vs.copy()
                height_seq = [float(x) for x in _scale_sequence_array(arr)]
            else:
                v_height = _upsample_v(vs, vs.size, n_full)
                height_seq = [float(x) for x in _scale_sequence_array(vs)]

    N_full = _next_pow2(3 * n_full - 2)
    best_full_v = None
    best_full_score = np.inf

    def current_best():
        return best_full_v, best_full_score

    if v_height is not None:
        sc_h = _score_v(v_height, N_full, n_full)
        if np.isfinite(sc_h):
            best_full_v = v_height.copy()
            best_full_score = float(sc_h)
            print(f"height_sequence_1 internal score={sc_h:.7f} n={n_full}")

    if best_full_v is None:
        best_full_v = np.zeros(n_full, dtype=np.float64)
        best_full_score = 2.0
        print("starting from uniform fallback n=%d" % n_full)

    n_small = max(256, min(1024, int(n_full // 8) if n_full // 8 > 256 else 256))
    N_small = _next_pow2(3 * n_small - 2)

    small_limit = min(deadline - 900.0, t0 + 720.0)
    if small_limit < t0 + 60.0:
        small_limit = min(deadline, t0 + 60.0)
    gen_deadline = min(small_limit - 300.0, t0 + 75.0)
    if gen_deadline < t0 + 5.0:
        gen_deadline = t0 + 5.0

    small_cands = _generate_candidates(n_small, N_small, rng, v_source=v_height, deadline=gen_deadline)
    if not small_cands:
        small_cands = [(2.0, np.zeros(n_small, dtype=np.float64))]
    small_cands.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
    print(f"small candidates n={n_small}: best_initial={small_cands[0][0]:.7f} count={len(small_cands)}")

    selected = []
    if small_cands:
        msc = len(small_cands)
        for i in range(min(8, msc)):
            selected.append(i)
        if msc > 8:
            pool = list(range(min(50, msc)))
            try:
                choices = rng.choice(pool, size=min(4, len(pool)), replace=False)
            except Exception:
                choices = []
            for idx in choices:
                idx = int(idx)
                if idx not in selected:
                    selected.append(idx)

    small_results = []
    for rank, idx in enumerate(selected):
        if time.monotonic() >= small_limit - 5.0:
            break
        sc, vv = small_cands[idx]
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        remaining = small_limit - time.monotonic()
        left = len(selected) - rank
        slice_time = min(80.0, max(8.0, remaining / max(1, left)))
        end = time.monotonic() + slice_time
        v2, sc2 = _local_search(vv, sc, n_small, N_small, 8, end, rng, K_max=32, print_every=3000)
        tiny_best = min(float(sc), float(sc2))
        small_results.append((tiny_best, v2))

    if small_results:
        small_results.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))
        print(f"small local top score={small_results[0][0]:.7f}")

    full_cands = []
    if v_height is not None:
        vf = _upsample_v(v_height, v_height.size if v_height.size > 1 else 1, n_full)
        scf = _score_v(vf, N_full, n_full)
        if np.isfinite(scf):
            full_cands.append((float(scf), vf))
            if scf < best_full_score:
                best_full_score = float(scf)
                best_full_v = vf.copy()

    for sc, vs in small_results[:8]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240.0 and len(full_cands) >= 3:
            break
        vf = _upsample_v(vs, n_small, n_full)
        scf = _score_v(vf, N_full, n_full)
        if np.isfinite(scf):
            full_cands.append((float(scf), vf))
            if scf < best_full_score:
                best_full_score = float(scf)
                best_full_v = vf.copy()

    if not full_cands:
        vf = _random_smooth_perturb(n_full, rng, 0.6)
        vf = _center_clip(vf)
        scf = _score_v(vf, N_full, n_full)
        if np.isfinite(scf):
            full_cands.append((float(scf), vf))
            if scf < best_full_score:
                best_full_score = float(scf)
                best_full_v = vf.copy()
        else:
            full_cands.append((2.0, np.zeros(n_full, dtype=np.float64)))
            best_full_v = np.zeros(n_full, dtype=np.float64)
            best_full_score = 2.0

    full_cands.sort(key=operator.itemgetter(0))

    full_deadline = deadline - 25.0
    num_full = min(4, len(full_cands))
    for rank in range(num_full):
        if time.monotonic() >= full_deadline - 60.0:
            break
        remaining = full_deadline - time.monotonic()
        left = num_full - rank
        slice_time = min(700.0, max(80.0, remaining / max(1, left)))
        end = time.monotonic() + slice_time
        sc, vv = full_cands[rank]
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        v2, sc2 = _local_search(vv, sc, n_full, N_full, 16, end, rng, K_max=128, print_every=800)
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_full_score:
            best_full_score = float(sc2)
            best_full_v = v2.copy()
            print(f"full local improved -> {best_full_score:.7f}")

    if time.monotonic() + 180.0 < deadline:
        vv, ss = _local_search(
            best_full_v,
            best_full_score,
            n_full,
            N_full,
            16,
            deadline - 6.0,
            rng,
            K_max=128,
            print_every=1500,
        )
        if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < best_full_score:
            best_full_score = float(ss)
            best_full_v = vv.copy()

    seq_best = _v_to_sequence(best_full_v)
    candidates = [seq_best]
    if height_seq is not None and len(height_seq) == len(seq_best):
        candidates.append(height_seq)

    final_seq = seq_best
    final_score = best_full_score

    if len(seq_best) <= 12000:
        for seq in candidates:
            try:
                sc = _score_sequence_direct(seq)
            except Exception:
                sc = np.inf
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
                final_score = float(sc)
                final_seq = seq
    else:
        eval_fn = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        if callable(eval_fn):
            for seq in candidates:
                try:
                    sc = float(eval_fn(seq))
                except Exception:
                    sc = np.inf
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < final_score:
                    final_score = float(sc)
                    final_seq = seq

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        eval_fn = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
        if callable(eval_fn):
            try:
                final_score = float(eval_fn(seq_best))
            except Exception:
                final_score = np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(final_score):
            final_score = _score_sequence_direct(seq_best)
            final_seq = seq_best

    if not isinstance(final_seq, list):
        final_seq = [float(x) for x in final_seq]

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        final_seq = [1.0] * n_full

    print(f"final internal/approx score={final_score:.7f} length={len(final_seq)}")
    return final_seq
```
